| auth_rls_initplan            | Auth RLS Initialization Plan | WARN  | EXTERNAL | ["PERFORMANCE"] | Detects if calls to \`current_setting()\` and \`auth.<function>()\` in RLS policies are being unnecessarily re-evaluated for each row                                                                                                                       | Table \`public.profiles\` has a row level security policy \`Allow insert on profiles table\` that re-evaluates current_setting() or auth.<function>() for each row.
